**[Japan](15573.md)** is hosting the **[2026 Asian Games](51790406.md)** in the [Aichi Prefecture](524618.md) and [Nagoya](61720.md) from 19 September to 4 October 2026.

Japan is expected to field more than 900 athletes which is larger than the delegation in the [2022 Asian Games](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_Asian_Games) which had 772 athletes.[^cite_note-1]

Further information: [Archery at the 2026 Asian Games](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Archery_at_the_2026_Asian_Games)

## Basketball

Main article: [Basketball at the 2026 Asian Games](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Basketball_at_the_2026_Asian_Games)

Both of Japan's [men's](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Japan_men's_national_basketball_team) and [women's national basketball teams](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Japan_women's_national_basketball_team) will play at the 2026 Asian Games.[^cite_note-bkbjp26-4] Japan will send a secondary team in the women's team event coached by [Yuko Oga](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yuko_Oga)[^cite_note-5], since the main team coached by [Corey Gaines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Corey_Gaines) has commitments to the [2026 FIBA Women's Basketball World Cup](73471455.md).[^cite_note-6]

## Esports

Main article: [Esports at the 2026 Asian Games](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Esports_at_the_2026_Asian_Games)

The Japan eSports Union confirmed that Japan will participate in seven out of nine events in [esports](564204.md). ## Volleyball

Main article: [Volleyball at the 2026 Asian Games](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Volleyball_at_the_2026_Asian_Games)

Both the [men's](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Japan_men's_national_volleyball_team) and [women's national volleyball teams](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Japan_women's_national_volleyball_team) of Japan have qualified for the indoor volleyball tournaments of the 2026 Asian Games. They earned their place via [FIVB](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/F%C3%A9d%C3%A9ration_Internationale_de_Volleyball) rankings.[^cite_note-qualify-20]

## Wrestling

Main article: [Wrestling at the 2026 Asian Games](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wrestling_at_the_2026_Asian_Games)

Japan entered twelve male and six female wrestlers.[^cite_note-21]

**Key**:

- VT (ranking points: 5–0 or 0–5) – [Victory by fall](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pin_\(amateur_wrestling\)).

- VB (ranking points: 5–0 or 0–5) – Victory by injury (VF for forfeit, VA for withdrawal or disqualification)

- PP (ranking points: 3–1 or 1–3) – Decision by points – the loser with technical points.

- PO (ranking points: 3–0 or 0–3) – Decision by points – the loser without technical points.

- ST (ranking points: 4–0 or 0–4) – Great superiority – the loser without technical points and a margin of victory of at least 8 (Greco-Roman) or 10 (freestyle) points.

- SP (ranking points: 4–1 or 1–4) – Technical superiority – the loser with technical points and a margin of victory of at least 8 (Greco-Roman) or 10 (freestyle) points.
